--- Caption ---
Two ways to build a home: on site, or in a system.

On site, weather and conditions change daily and every build starts fresh. In a system, conditions are controlled and processes repeat. The way I see it: less happens on site, so less can go wrong there.

Save the 5 questions for comparing how to build.

Which would you choose for your home?
